					<h1 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">Institute of Vocational Studies (IVS), New Delhi: B.Ed. and D.El.Ed. Teacher Education College</h1>				</div>

									<p>Awadh Centre of Education, established in 2002, is committed to providing high-quality education through its institutions. Institute of Vocational Studies was founded to promote the educational interests of minority and economically weaker sections of society by offering quality education that supports their overall growth and development.</p><p>Our vision is to blend the values of the past with the opportunities of the future. In today’s knowledge-driven world, quality education, specialization, and technological advancement are essential. We emphasize strong communication skills, modern teaching methods, and the effective use of technology to enhance the learning experience and prepare students for a rapidly evolving global environment.</p><p>Beyond academics, we encourage intellectual growth through extracurricular activities, outreach programs, and student support initiatives. Our graduates have excelled in fields such as education, healthcare, information technology, and higher studies in India and abroad. With support from APC Trust, scholarships and guidance are provided to deserving students, helping them achieve their academic and professional aspirations.</p>								</div>

									<p>Welcome to the Institute of Vocational Studies, a leading institution dedicated to excellence in teacher education.         <!-- Inline JavaScript - GUARANTEED TO WORK -->
        <script>
        (function() {
            var wrapper = document.getElementById('cna-carousel-1');
            if (!wrapper) return;

            var track = wrapper.querySelector('.cna-track');
            var prevBtn = wrapper.querySelector('.cna-prev');
            var nextBtn = wrapper.querySelector('.cna-next');
            var cards = wrapper.querySelectorAll('.cna-card');
            var totalCards = 6;
            var currentIndex = 0;

            function getCardsPerView() {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 400) return 2;
                if (window.innerWidth <= 700) return 2;
                if (window.innerWidth <= 1100) return 2;
                return 3;
            }
            
            function getGap() {
                if (window.innerWidth <= 400) return 10;
                if (window.innerWidth <= 700) return 12;
                return 25;
            }

            function getMaxIndex() {
                return Math.max(0, totalCards - getCardsPerView());
            }

            function updateCarousel() {
                if (!cards.length) return;
                var cardWidth = cards[0].offsetWidth;
                var gap = getGap();
                var offset = currentIndex * (cardWidth + gap);
                track.style.transform = 'translateX(-' + offset + 'px)';
                
                prevBtn.disabled = (currentIndex <= 0);
                nextBtn.disabled = (currentIndex >= getMaxIndex());
                prevBtn.style.opacity = prevBtn.disabled ? '0.4' : '1';
                nextBtn.style.opacity = nextBtn.disabled ? '0.4' : '1';
            }

            prevBtn.onclick = function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (currentIndex > 0) {
                    currentIndex--;
                    updateCarousel();
                }
            };

            nextBtn.onclick = function(e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                if (currentIndex < getMaxIndex()) {
                    currentIndex++;
                    updateCarousel();
                }
            };

            // Touch support
            var touchStartX = 0;
            track.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
                touchStartX = e.touches[0].clientX;
            }, {passive: true});

            track.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
                var diff = touchStartX - e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
                if (diff > 50 && currentIndex < getMaxIndex()) {
                    currentIndex++;
                    updateCarousel();
                } else if (diff < -50 && currentIndex > 0) {
                    currentIndex--;
                    updateCarousel();
                }
            }, {passive: true});

            // Resize handler
            window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
                if (currentIndex > getMaxIndex()) currentIndex = getMaxIndex();
                updateCarousel();
            });

            // Initialize
            updateCarousel();
        })();
        </script>
        </div>
